```json
    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",
```

````text
{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"worker_id\": \"worker_investigate_lora-alpha-bug_20260515T150214\", \"status\": \"done\", \"task_type\": \"investigate:lora-alpha-bug\", \"project\": \"contamcal-irt-calibration\", \"started_at\": \"2026-05-15T15:02:15.075057+00:00\", \"ended_at\": \"2026-05-15T15:05:20.761905+00:00\", \"setup\": null, \"teardown\": null, \"setup_data\": {}, \"result\": {\"raw\": \"调查完成，以下是结论。\\n\\n---\\n\\n## 调查报告：`--lora-alpha` 参数传递问题\\n\\n### 结论：`train_sft.py` 本身无 bug\\n\\nargparse 定义和传递链完全正确：\\n\\n```\\n--lora-alpha → dest=lora_alpha → args.lora_alpha → LoraConfig(lora_alpha=args.lora_alpha)\\n```\\n\\n代码逻辑无误，`--lora-alpha 16` 如果被正确传入，会按预期工作。\\n\\n### 真正的问题\\n\\n**`exp_001_sft_3B_d025_s123` 的实际提交命令没有传入 `--lora-alpha 16`**（或该参数被忽略/遗漏），导致使用了 `DEFAULT_LORA_CONFIG[\\\"lora_alpha\\\"] = 32` 的默认值。\\n\\n三重证据确认：\\n1. **训练日志**：`LoRA config: r=16, alpha=32`\\n2. **train_config.json**：`\\\"lora_alpha\\\": 32`\\n3. **adapter_config.json**：`\\\"lora_alpha\\\": 32`\\n\\n### 全量影响扫描（26 个实验）\\n\\n| alpha 值 | 实验数 | 涉及模型 |\\n|---------|--------|---------|\\n| **32**（默认值）| **21** | 全部 0.5B (8), 全部 1.5B (8), 3B d000/d005/d025_s123 (5) |\\n| **16**（手动指定）| **5** | 3B d000_v2 (2), 3B d025_s42 (1), 3B d100 (2) |\\n\\n**关键问题：alpha 在实验间不一致。** 同一 dosage 的不同 seed 之间 alpha 不同（如 3B_d025_s42 用 16，3B_d025_s123 用 32），这会污染 seed 间的可比性。\\n\\n### 附加发现\\n\\n`run_exp001.sh` 中的训练调用：\\n```bash\\npython3 \\\"$SCRIPT_DIR/train_sft.py\\\" \\\\\\n    --model-name \\\"$model\\\" \\\\\\n    --data-path \\\"$data_file\\\" \\\\\\n    --output-dir \\\"$run_dir\\\" \\\\\\n    --seed \\\"$seed\\\" \\\\\\n    --gpu \\\"$gpu_id\\\" \\\\       # ← train_sft.py 没有 --gpu 参数\\n    --cache-dir \\\"$CACHE_DIR\\\"\\n    # ← 没有 --lora-alpha，没有 --lora-r\\n```\\n\\n脚本没有暴露 LoRA 超参数，且传了一个不存在的 `--gpu` 参数（说明实验可能不是通过此脚本提交的，而是手动/任务系统单独提交）。\\n\\n### 建议\\n\\n不需要修 `train_sft.py`（代码正确）。需要决定的是：\\n\\n1. **alpha=32 和 alpha=16 哪个是实验的 intended 值？** 这决定了哪些实验需要重跑\\n2. 如果 intended 是 16，可以把 `DEFAULT_LORA_CONFIG[\\\"lora_alpha\\\"]` 从 32 改为 16，减少未来遗漏的风险\\n3. `run_exp001.sh` 的 `run_train()` 需要补上 `--lora-alpha` 参数转发\\n\\n需要我改默认值或修 `run_exp001.sh` 吗？\"}}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {}}"}
````

```text
Let me process the Worker's findings and the current state:

## Worker Report Summary
The worker investigated the `--lora-alpha` bug in `train_sft.py`:
- **Code is correct** — no bug in `train_sft.py` itself
- **The problem**: `exp_001_sft_3B_d025_s123` was submitted without `--lora-alpha 16`, so it used the default value of 32
- **Impact**: 21 out of 26 experiments used alpha=32 (default), 5 used alpha=16 (manually specified)


- **Critical issue**: Same dosage with different seeds have inconsistent alpha values (like 3B_d025_s42 got 16 while 3B_d025_s123 got 32), breaking seed-level comparisons
- **Script problem**: `run_exp001.sh` doesn't expose LoRA hyperparameters and passes an invalid `--gpu` parameter

## Current State
- 7 experiments still RUNNING (crons confirmed)
- 155 total experiments, 128 DONE
- 6 GPUs allocated, cuda:2 appears idle
- Deadline: EMNLP 2026 ARR May 25 (10 days away)

## Key Findings
The alpha inconsistency is a serious data integrity problem that could undermine the multi-seed pooling analysis outlined in the roadmap. I need to determine which experiments are affected and whether this invalidates our conclusions, then update the registry and brief the Director on the findings and next steps. The Worker's analysis shows alpha varies between seeds for identical model/dosage combinations, which is critical since the roadmap depends on seed=123 completion to trigger multi-seed pooled IRT analysis with N≥16. I'm weighing whether to standardize on alpha=16 or alpha=32 going forward.

Before reporting to the Director, I should review the resource-saturation skill and check the current experiment status. Looking at what's running now, I see several experiments active across different GPUs and one sweep job that doesn't appear on the cron list in the report, which I should clarify. The completed experiment used alpha=32 instead of the intended 16, so the model was trained with incorrect hyperparameters. I need to prepare the Director report covering this finding, the current resource allocation, and my recommendations, starting with reviewing the resource-saturation documentation.
```

```text
The registry doesn't consistently track lora_alpha in hyperparams. Only 2 experiments have it recorded: `exp_001_sft_05B_d005_s123` (alpha=32) and `exp_001_sft_d100_3B_v2` (alpha=16).

The Worker's investigation was based on actually checking the training logs and config files on the server, which is more authoritative. The Worker found:
- 21/26 experiments used alpha=32 (default)
- 5/26 used alpha=16 (manually specified): 3B d000_v2 (2), 3B d025_s42 (1), 3B d100 (2)


- 
Now I need to verify that multi-seed pooling won't mix experiments with different alpha values for the same model/dosage combination. I should report this hyperparameter discrepancy to the Director and then check the current GPU allocation status. Looking at the resource state, I have 6 GPUs allocated on cqa1-21977 with 7 running experiments, though cuda:2 seems idle and one experiment isn't showing a GPU assignment. I need to report the lora-alpha inconsistency to the Director and get a decision on standardization, while also addressing the idle cuda:2 GPU and spawning additional workers to meet the minimum pool requirement. I should also clarify what needs to happen with the experiment currently in ANALYZING state.
```

```text
I should be proactive here — report the alpha issue to Director while also spawning workers to keep the pool active, since having fewer than 2 workers running would be neglecting my responsibilities. I'm getting tangled up in whether that last data point is s42 or s123, but the Worker already gave me the full breakdown.
```
